- **onboarding-loop**: Use this skill to prepare onboarding and lifecycle email for new and dormant hosted sign-ups in prepare-and-approve mode: a guided first-value message for a fresh sign-up, an activation nudge for someone who connected but has not run a report, and a re-engagement draft for a dormant account. It prepares Gmail drafts only; it never sends. Distinct from the agency `client-onboarding` skill, which records a client's advisory strategy. Trigger on: "prepare onboarding emails", "draft activation nudges", "welcome new sign-ups", "re-engage dormant hosted users", "run the onboarding loop". - **one-by-one**: 逐题裁决模式（用户级通用）。仅由用户手动输入 $one-by-one 触发，AI 永不主动激活。触发后本会话禁用弹窗式提问，改为纯文本逐题裁决：一题一讲、一裁一记录、记完再下一题；表达用通俗汉语技术方案语言，禁 AI 黑话。典型触发场景：用户取消了弹窗、看不懂 AI 的提问或解释、要求逐个讨论决策点。 ([BackToCimaCoppi/Praxis](https://github.com/BackToCimaCoppi/Praxis/tree/HEAD/skills/one-by-one) · ★6)
